\f0\b\fs36 \cf0 4. Branch Scope and Production Context\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 The Commercial branch covers TapeCoach\'92s operational 
\f2\fs26 commercial
\f1\fs24  audition type and its use of the shared six-field model, especially the 
\f2\fs26 acting
\f1\fs24  field as Commercial presence / naturalism. The completed Commercial refinement is intended to improve Commercial report specificity, field semantics, evidence anchoring, claim-scope control, self-tape assessability handling, professional presentation boundaries, accessibility-safe language and non-regression testing.\
The revised Commercial outputs are proposed branch improvements. They are not yet shipped production behaviour unless a later implementation and rollout explicitly applies them.\
Current live production behaviour remains separate from the research package. The live product continues to use the existing architecture, fields, weights, pipeline, schemas, scrubs and rendering system. This branch must not change those directly.\
Commercial must preserve:\
\pard\tx220\tx720\pardeftab720\li720\fi-720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\cf0 Adversarial / non-regression tests: COMMERCIAL-OS-T01\'96T31 cover direct-to-camera, reader-scene, no-dialogue, no-brief, supplied copy/product, generic praise, mixed copy/camera evidence, poor lighting, poor audio, domestic capture, high polish/weak performance, reader resource, live-room overclaims, process/admin as talent, voiceover conditionality, unsupported subtypes, marketability/look language, access contexts, presentation polish, comparison mismatch, timestamp underproduction and safety/consent boundaries.\
Display-layer checks: Commercial category labels, 
\f2\fs26 acting
\f1\fs24  semantics, irrelevant Vocal suppression, component/category breakdown wording, comparison score/label parity, timestamp count/order/specificity, export parity, feedback reliability, professional_presentation wording, no-brief/role-fit display and risk flag wording.\
Accessibility / anti-bias protections: COMMERCIAL-OS-R16 and R17 must block access, disability, visual impairment, Deaf/disabled access, hearing difference, speech difference, accent, gender-diverse voice, mobility difference, convalescence, adaptation, appearance, body type, \'93look\'94, bookability and marketability as deficit or talent evidence.\
Timestamp expectations: COMMERCIAL-OS-R20 requires chronological, evidence-led timestamps tied to copy, camera, tone, physical/non-dialogue evidence or assessability, within the preserved maximum of 8.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa298\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs36 \cf0 15. Compact Branch Handoff Pack\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Commercial is mature enough for cross-branch consolidation. The branch is complete through source census, targeted extraction, synthesis, gap audit, revision, final audit, output-specificity mapping and implementation-readiness handoff. The stable ID families are COMMERCIAL-S001\'96S030, COMMERCIAL-B1-F01\'96F09, COMMERCIAL-B2-F01\'96F09, COMMERCIAL-B3-F01\'96F10, COMMERCIAL-B4-F01\'96F06, COMMERCIAL-SYN-F01\'96F24, COMMERCIAL-AUDIT-I01\'96I17, COMMERCIAL-AUDIT-R01\'96R21, COMMERCIAL-REV-C01\'96C21, COMMERCIAL-REV-S01\'96S21, COMMERCIAL-FINAL-D01\'96D02, COMMERCIAL-OS-R01\'96R21, COMMERCIAL-OS-T01\'96T31 and COMMERCIAL-OS-I01\'96I15.\
The strongest evidence base comes from B1, B2 and B3. B1 establishes Commercial as distinct from theatre acting and generic screen acting, with evidence around grounded reality, task-calibrated tone, copy handling and camera relationship. B2 establishes assessability and anti-polish: Commercial feedback is only reliable when the tape is readable, audible and task-appropriate, and simple domestic capture can be acceptable. B3 establishes the official process, fairness, safety and access spine: reader access, deadlines, upload burden, paid resources, nudity/stunt/privacy/consent, protected characteristics and accessibility must not become talent evidence. B4 is conditional only, mainly supporting narrow voiceover-style copy analysis.\
COMMERCIAL-AUDIT found the baseline directionally correct but too generic. COMMERCIAL-REV addressed this through wording and guardrail revisions only, preserving all score fields, weights, caps, blockers, verdict thresholds, role-fit bounds, schema, backend, pipeline, report schema and the MT acting + song anchor. COMMERCIAL-FINAL-AUDIT approved the revision with explicit limitations. COMMERCIAL-OUTPUT-SPEC created COMMERCIAL-OS-R01\'96R21 and COMMERCIAL-OS-T01\'96T31.\
Commercial is implementation-ready as a production-facing requirement package, not as shipped production behaviour. The key implementation-readiness implications are: clarify 
\f2\fs26 acting
\f1\fs24  as Commercial presence/naturalism through observable copy, camera, tone and product/situation evidence; block generic praise; prevent no-brief brand/product/audience invention; separate direct-to-camera from reader-scene work; treat assessability as a precondition; block polish/resource/marketability/look/bookability evidence; and require access-safe, non-deficit language.\
Not verified: live Commercial output quality, frontend labels, comparison labels, renderer/export timestamp parity, persisted-versus-rendered timestamps and score stability. No Commercial branch patch is required before consolidation, but live Commercial examples are required before product release sign-off.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa298\partightenfactor0
